Part G — Lecture 6

Neural Networks

Ye hissa Lecture-6 se hai. OHT-II sample paper ke Q-1, Q-2 aur Q-3 seedha isi se aaye hain — yaani 60 mein se 18 marks. Is Part mein do cheezein hain: pehli, neural network ka structure (G1–G12); doosri, perceptron (G13–G17). Dono mein numerical sawal aate hain, is liye G8 aur G10 ko do do baar hal karein.

G0Notation cheat sheet — pehle ye yaad karein

Lecture-6 mein superscript (oopar ka number) aur subscript (neeche ka number) dono aate hain, aur yehi sab se bada confusion hai. Qaida seedha hai: oopar ka number = kaunsi layer, neeche ka number = us layer ke andar kaunsa neuron. Ye ek baat pakad lein to poora lecture khul jaayega.

SymbolPadha jaata haiIska matlab Roman Urdu mein
xxInput feature vector. Jo network ke andar jaata hai. x ∈ Rd — yaani d numbers ki list.
ddInput mein kitne numbers hain (input dimension).
MMLayers ki total ginti. M-layer network.
gig superscript iLayer number i. Ek function — pichhli layer ka output leta hai, apna output deta hai.
ziz superscript iLayer i ka output — isay activation ya output of layer i kehte hain. zi ∈ Rdi.
did sub iLayer i mein kitne neurons hain (us layer ka output dimension).
z0z zero= x. Yaani input ko bhi "layer 0 ka output" maan liya jaata hai. Is liye d0 = d.
zMz superscript M= ŷ. Aakhri layer ka output = model ka jawab. Is liye dM = m (output dimension).
Θicapital theta superscript iLayer i ka parameter matrix. Size (di-1 + 1) × di. Wo +1 bias ke liye hai — yaad rakhein, ye sab se zyada marks waali cheez hai.
θthetaPoore network ke saare parameters. θ = (Θ1, …, ΘM) — har layer ka matrix ek jagah jama.
hhActivation function. h : R → R — ek number leta hai, ek number deta hai. Vector par lagao to har entry par alag alag (elementwise) lagta hai.
W ya wW / wWeights. Connection ki taqat — ek input kitna important hai.
bbBias. Neuron ka apna offset — input chahe zero ho, phir bhi neuron ke paas apni ek value hoti hai.
aaPre-activation ya activation level. a = Σ xiwi — activation function lagne se pehle ki value.
ttThreshold. Perceptron mein wo had jis se oopar neuron "fire" karta hai.
ηetaLearning rate. Ek update mein weights kitna hilenge. Chhota = aahista magar mehfooz.
°circ / compositionFunction composition. g2 ° g1 ka matlab "pehle g1 chalao, uska jawab g2 ko do". Dayein se bayein padhein.
(·)+plus part(a)+ = max(a, 0). Negative ko zero bana do, positive ko waisa hi rakho. Yehi ReLU hai.

Ek jumle mein poora Lecture-6

Neural network koi jaadu nahi hai. Ye bas yehi kaam baar baar karta hai:

(1) numbers ko weights se multiply kar ke jama karo → affine (linear + bias) (2) natije ko ek tedhe function se guzaro → activation h (3) jo mila, usay agli layer ka input bana do → composition x → [affine] → [h] → z1 → [affine] → [h] → z2 → … → y-hat

Step (1) seekhne wala hissa hai (weights/bias). Step (2) fixed hai (ReLU ya sigmoid — is mein koi parameter nahi). Step (3) sirf "repeat" hai. Bas itna hi hai.

G1Neural network kya hai — definition

Slide ki definition yaad karne ke qabil hai, kyunki paper mein "what is a neural network" nahi poocha jaata, balki "why is a neural network called a nonlinear predictor" ya "what advantage does it have over a linear predictor" poocha jaata hai — aur un ka jawab isi definition mein hai.

Ek neural network (NN) ek NONLINEAR predictor y-hat = g(x) hai, jiski ek KHAAS LAYERED SHAKAL hoti hai.

Is mein do lafz ahem hain:

  • Nonlinear — yaani linear predictor ŷ = θTx se zyada tedhe (complex) rishte seekh sakta hai. Ye nonlinearity sirf activation function h se aati hai (dekhein G7).
  • Layered form — ye koi bhi nonlinear function nahi, balki ek khaas tarteeb mein jama kiya gaya hai: layer ke oopar layer.

Slides par NN ki paanch khaas baatein likhi hain — ye seedha "advantages and limitations" wale sawal ka jawab hai:

BaatMatlabAcha ya bura
NN automatic feature engineering karta haiFeature banane ka kaam, jo pehle haath se karna padta tha, NN khud seekh leta hai — hidden layers hi naye features hainFaida
Parameter dimension θ bohat bada ho sakta haiLakhon ya crore parametersNuqsan — data aur memory chahiye
Training tricky hai aur lamba waqt leti haiOptimization problem convex nahi — ek hi best jawab ki zamanat nahiNuqsan
NN bohat acha chalta hai, khaas tor par jab data bohat zyada hoChhote data par simple model behtar reh sakta haiFaida (shart ke saath)
NN ko samajhna bohat mushkil haiLinear predictor mein θj dekh kar bata sakte hain ke feature j kitna important hai. NN mein ye mumkin nahiNuqsan — interpretability

Exam mein likhne ka jumla

"A neural network is a nonlinear predictor ŷ = gθ(x) formed as a composition of layers, each layer being a scalar activation function applied elementwise to an affine function of the previous layer's output. It performs data-driven automatic feature engineering, but has a very high parameter count and is hard to interpret, unlike a linear predictor where θj directly gives the effect of feature j."

G2Layers aur function composition

Feedforward neural network ka matlab: chand functions ko ek ke baad ek chala dena. Teen layers ke saath:

y-hat = g3( g2( g1(x) ) ) ya chhota kar ke: g = g3 ° g2 ° g1

Har gi ko ek layer kehte hain. Teen functions = teen layers. Aur ye jitni layers tak chala sakte hain — koi had nahi.

Composition ka nishaan ° dayein se bayein padha jaata hai. g3 ° g2 ° g1 mein sab se pehle g1 chalta hai (jo sab se dayein hai). Log yahan ulta samajh lete hain. Ek aasaan tareeqa: brackets dekhein — g1(x) sab se andar hai, aur andar waala pehle chalta hai, jaise maths mein BODMAS.

Is poori cheez ke do aur naam bhi hain, jo paper mein aa sakte hain:

Teen naam, ek hi cheez

  • Multi-layer perceptron (MLP)
  • Fully-connected network (FCN)
  • Feedforward neural network

Slide par saaf likha hai: "Neural Network" ek bohat aam (broad) lafz hai; jo cheez hum padh rahe hain usay theek tor par fully-connected network ya multi-layer perceptron kehna chahiye. Agar paper mein "MLP" likha ho, ghabrayein nahi — wohi hai.

Feedforward kyun kehte hain: kyunki data sirf aagay jaata hai — x se ŷ tak, kabhi peechay nahi. Koi loop nahi.

G3Activations zi aur layer dimensions di

Composition ko ek saath likhne ke bajaye, hum usay qadam ba qadam todte hain — aur yehi tareeqa numerical sawal mein kaam aata hai:

z1 = g1(x) z2 = g2(z1) y-hat = g3(z2)

Har zi ko do naam diye jaate hain — dono exam mein aa sakte hain:

  • Activation of layer i
  • Output of layer i

zi ∈ Rdi — yaani layer i ke output mein di numbers hain. Aur ye bohat ahem baat hai:

Zaroori baat

Layer output dimensions di ka ek jaisa hona zaroori nahi hai. Yaani network ki ek layer 32 neurons ki ho sakti hai aur agli 16 ki. Isi liye parameter counting mein har layer alag se ginni padti hai — ek formula se saari layers ka jawab nahi nikalta.

Likhne ki sahulat ke liye input aur output ko bhi "activation" maan liya jaata hai:

z0 = x aur d0 = d (input bhi layer 0 ka output) z3 = y-hat aur d3 = m (output bhi aakhri layer ka output)

Is se fayda ye hota hai ke ek hi formula saari layers par lag jaata hai — pehli layer ko koi khaas case banane ki zaroorat nahi.

Isay aksar flow graph ki shakal mein dikhaya jaata hai:

G4Layer function ka formula — sab se ahem formula

Ab asli baat. Har layer kya karti hai? Slide ka formula:

gi(zi-1) = h( (Θi)T (1, zi-1) ) , i = 1, …, M

Pehli nazar mein darawna hai. Isay teen tukdon mein todein:

  1. (1, zi-1) — pichhli layer ke output ke oopar ek 1 chipka do. Yaani agar zi-1 mein 5 numbers thay, ab 6 ho gaye: (1, z1, z2, z3, z4, z5). Is 1 ki wajah se bias aata hai — is ke saath jo weight multiply hoga, wohi bias hai.
  2. (Θi)T (·) — matrix se multiply karo. Ye affine hissa hai: weights × inputs, phir jama, aur bias bhi saath.
  3. h(·) — jo mila, us ki har entry par activation function lagao. Elementwise — yaani agar 16 numbers aaye to 16 baar alag alag h lagega, inhein mila kar nahi.

Parameter matrix ka size — ye formula exam mein sab se zyada marks laata hai:

Θi ∈ R(di-1 + 1) × di rows = di-1 + 1 ← pichhli layer ke neurons + 1 (wo "+1" bias hai) columns = di ← is layer ke neurons

Aur poore M-layer network ka parameter:

θ = ( Θ1, Θ2, …, ΘM )

Yaad rakhne ka tareeqa

Ek layer = "affine + tedha". Affine hissa (Θ) seekha jaata hai. Tedha hissa (h) pehle se tay hai — us mein koi parameter nahi hota. Is liye parameter ginte waqt h ko bilkul nazarandaz kar dein — ReLU ho ya sigmoid, parameter count par koi asar nahi padta.

G5Weights, biases aur "neuron" ki definition

Θi matrix ke andar do qism ki cheezein hain. Pehli row bias hai, baaqi saari rows weights hain:

+-----------------+ Θi = | (bi)T | ← pehli row : saare neurons ke BIAS | | | Wi | ← baaqi rows : WEIGHTS +-----------------+ jahan bi ∈ Rdi (is layer mein jitne neurons, utne bias) Wi ∈ Rdi-1 × di (pichhle neurons × is layer ke neurons)

Is tarteeb ki wajah se layer map yun khul jaata hai:

(Θi)T(1, zi-1) = bi + (Wi)T zi-1

Aur ek neuron ke level par (j-waan neuron):

zij = h( bij + (wij)T zi-1 ) , j = 1, …, di

Definition — yaad kar lein

Ek neuron ki tareef slide par ye hai:

"A composition of h with an affine function is called a neuron." Yaani: ek number banao (weights × inputs + bias), phir us par h lagao — ye poora kaam ek neuron hai. bij us neuron ka bias hai, aur wij uske (input) weights.

Weights aur bias ka farq — aasaan misaal. Socha jaaye ek neuron faisla kar raha hai "baarish hogi ya nahi":

  • Weights kehte hain: "humidity bohat important hai (weight bada), wind direction kam important hai (weight chhota)". Yaani weights batate hain kaunsa input kitna matter karta hai.
  • Bias kehta hai: "is ilaaqe mein baarish aam tor par hoti hi rehti hai, to main shuru se hi 'haan' ki taraf jhuka hua hoon". Yaani bias neuron ka apna jhukaav hai, inputs se qat'e-nazar. Saare inputs zero ho jaayein, bias phir bhi mojood rehta hai.

G6Activation function — ReLU aur sigmoid

Activation function h : R → R ek number leta hai aur ek number deta hai. Shart sirf ek hai: h nonlinear hona chahiye (kyun — G7 dekhein).

Slides par do diye hain. Dono ka formula aur shakal yaad karein:

NaamFormulaRangeKhaas baat
ReLU
(rectified linear unit)		h(a) = (a)+ = max(a, 0)[0, ∞)Negative ko zero kar deta hai, positive ko chhedta nahi. Slide: "ReLU is a good default choice for most problems"
Sigmoidh(a) = ea / (1 + ea)(0, 1)Har number ko 0 aur 1 ke darmiyan daba deta hai. S ki shakal (is liye "sigmoid").

Slides mein aur bhi naam gine gaye hain (sirf naam, tafseel nahi) — agar paper mein "name some activation functions" aa jaaye to ye likh dein:

Sigmoid · tanh · ReLU · Leaky ReLU · Maxout · ELU

ReLU ka amal — ek line mein

ReLU chowkidar hai. Jo value negative aayi, usay bilkul zero kar diya ("tum andar nahi aa sakte"). Jo positive aayi, usay bilkul waise hi jaane diya ("tum theek ho, jao"). Is liye max(0, −4.5) = 0 aur max(0, 2.0) = 2.0. Numerical sawal mein yehi ek line sab se zyada kaam aati hai.

G7Activation function hata dein to kya hoga?

Slide par ye sawal khud poocha gaya hai: "Q: What if we try to build a neural network without one?" aur jawab bhi diya hai: "A: We end up with a linear classifier again!"

Ye poore lecture ka sab se zyada aane wala conceptual sawal hai. Wajah samajh lein, kyunki "explain why" poocha jaata hai:

Farz karein h linear hai (ya bilkul nahi hai, yaani h(a) = a). Layer 1: z1 = W1T x Layer 2: z2 = W2T z1 = W2T (W1T x) = (W1 W2)T x Ab W1 W2 ek hi matrix hai — usay W keh dein: z2 = WT x ← ye LINEAR predictor hai!

Yaani: do affine functions ko jama karne se phir ek affine function hi banta hai. Chahe 100 layers laga dein, jawab ek hi matrix multiplication ke barabar hoga. Saari layers dhah kar ek ho jaayengi (isay "collapse" kehte hain).

Exam jawab — 2 line

"Without a nonlinear activation, each layer is affine, and a composition of affine maps is again affine: W2T(W1Tx) = (W1W2)Tx. The whole network therefore collapses to a single linear predictor, no matter how many layers are stacked — so it gains no expressive power and cannot separate data that is not linearly separable. The nonlinearity h is the only source of the network's expressive power."

Yeh baat diagram se dekhein. Slides par ek misaal hai: red aur blue points jinhein koi bhi seedhi line alag nahi kar sakti. Magar agar unhein pehle transform kar dein (cartesian se polar: f(x,y) = (r(x,y), θ(x,y))), to wo alag ho jaate hain:

G8Parameter counting — (di-1+1) × di

Ye topic OHT-II sample paper ka Q-2 hai. Taqreeban yaqeeni hai ke is qism ka ek sawal aayega. Isay zubani yaad karne ke bajaye tareeqa yaad karein — phir koi bhi number de dein, jawab nikal aayega.

Formula — ek layer ke liye:

Layer i ke parameters = (di-1 + 1) × di = di-1 × di (WEIGHTS) + di (BIASES)

Pehle layer dimensions ko naam do — d0, d1, d2, …

Sir slides mein hidden layers ko d1, d2 likhte hain. Ye koi nayi cheez nahi — bas har layer ke neurons ki ginti ka naam hai. Sawal mein numbers alfaaz mein diye hote hain; sab se pehla kaam unhein d ki shakal mein likhna hai:

"input 12, hidden 32, hidden 16, output 4 classes" ↓ naam do ↓ d0 = 12 ← INPUT (= d, feature count) d1 = 32 ← hidden 1 d2 = 16 ← hidden 2 dM = d3 = 4 ← OUTPUT (= m, classes)

Qaida: d0 hamesha input hai aur dM hamesha output. Beech waale saare hidden hain. Aur layers (matrices) ki ginti = d ki ginti − 1 — yahan 4 dimensions, to 3 layers. Ek baar ye likh lein, formula khud ba khud lag jaata hai.

Logic bilkul seedha hai, is liye isay rata lagane ki zaroorat nahi:

  • Weights: is layer ka har neuron, pichhli layer ke har neuron se juda hua hai (is liye "fully connected"). To di-1 × di taarein, yaani utne hi weights.
  • Biases: is layer ke har neuron ka apna ek bias hai. Neurons di hain, to biases bhi di.
  • Dono jama: di-1di + di = (di-1 + 1)di. Wo +1 bias ka hai — aur yehi wo cheez hai jo log bhool jaate hain.

Poore network ke liye: har layer ka alag alag nikalo, phir jama kar do.

Mukammal hal shuda misaal (sample paper Q-2)

Network: d0 = 12, d1 = 32, d2 = 16, d3 = 4 classes. Har neuron mein bias hai.

Layer 1 (d0→d1): (12 + 1) × 32 = 13 × 32 = 416 [384 weights + 32 biases] Layer 2 (d1→d2): (32 + 1) × 16 = 33 × 16 = 528 [512 weights + 16 biases] Layer 3 (d2→d3): (16 + 1) × 4 = 17 × 4 = 68 [ 64 weights + 4 biases] ----- TOTAL trainable parameters = 1,012

Chaar galtiyan jo log karte hain: (1) +1 bhool jaate hain → jawab 960 aata hai, ghalat. (2) Input ko ek "layer" gin lete hain aur ek extra matrix bana dete hain — input layer mein koi parameter nahi hota. (3) Activation function ke parameter gin lete hain — ReLU/sigmoid mein koi parameter nahi. (4) Output layer ko chhod dete hain — wo bhi poora layer hai, uske bhi weights aur bias hain.

Ek chhota drill — khud kar ke dekhein. d = 5, hidden = 10, output = 3, bias ke saath:

Jawab
Layer 1: (5 + 1) × 10 = 60 Layer 2: (10 + 1) × 3 = 33 Total = 93 parameters (weights 50 + 30 = 80, biases 10 + 3 = 13)

Aur agar bias na ho (jaise sample paper ke Q-1 mein "without biases" likha tha)? To +1 hata dein: 5×10 + 10×3 = 50 + 30 = 80. Sawal mein "without biases" ya "includes a learnable bias" dhyan se padhein — poora jawab isi ek lafz par badal jaata hai.

G9Layer ginne ka tareeqa — exam ka sab se bada trap

Ye sun kar bura lagta hai magar haqeeqat hai: "2-layer neural network" ka matlab hai ek hidden layer. Slides par dono naam saath likhe hain:

Jo likha jaata haiDoosra naamHidden layersParameter matrices
"2-layer Neural Net""1-hidden-layer Neural Net"12 — Θ1, Θ2
"3-layer Neural Net""2-hidden-layer Neural Net"23 — Θ1, Θ2, Θ3

Wajah: layers ginte waqt input ko nahi ginte, kyunki input koi hisaab nahi karta — wo sirf numbers hain jo andar aa rahe hain. Jo layers hisaab karti hain (weights × input + bias, phir activation), sirf wo ginti mein aati hain. Hidden layers + output layer = total layers.

Sample paper mein yehi trap tha

Q-1 mein likha tha: "A 2-layer fully connected feedforward network (one hidden layer) without biases". Examiner ne bracket mein khud khol diya — magar agar na kholta, to "2-layer" se do matrices (W1, W2) samajhna tha, teen nahi. Aur f(x) = W2 max(0, W1x) mein dekhein — waqai do hi matrices hain. Qaida: matrices ki ginti = layers ki ginti.

Aik aur baat slides par hai jo "why use many neurons" wale sawal ka jawab hai: "Learn 100 templates instead of 10. Share templates between classes." Yaani hidden layer ke neurons ko templates (naqshe) samjhein — linear classifier ke paas har class ke liye sirf ek template hota hai, magar hidden layer mein 100 templates ban sakte hain, aur mukhtalif classes unhein mil kar istemaal kar sakti hain. Isi se NN zyada taqatwar hota hai.

G10Forward pass — mukammal drill

Ye OHT-II ka Q-1 hai. Forward pass ka matlab: x ko andar daalo aur ŷ tak pohonchao, qadam ba qadam. Numerical sawal mein ghalti sirf tarteeb ki hoti hai, hisaab ki nahi — is liye tarteeb rata lein:

Forward pass ke 4 qadam — hamesha isi tarteeb se

QADAM 1 z = W₁ x ← pre-activation nikalo (matrix × vector) QADAM 2 h = ReLU(z) ← HAR entry par alag alag max(0, ·) lagao QADAM 3 f = W₂ h ← agli layer, phir matrix × vector QADAM 4 agar aur layer hain, to QADAM 2 par wapas jao

Sab se aam ghalti: qadam 2 chhod dena, yaani z ko hi aage bhej dena bina ReLU lagaye. Doosri aam ghalti: ReLU poore vector par "jama kar ke" lagana — nahi, har number par alag.

Matrix × vector kaise karte hain — agar ye bhool gaye hain to yahan se dekh lein. Qaida: row ko vector ke saath jod kar jama karo.

┌ ┐ ┌ ┐ ┌ ┐ │ 1.5 1.0 │ │ 2.0│ = │ 1.5(2.0) + 1.0(-1.0) │ = ┌ 2.0 ┐ │-2.0 0.5 │ │-1.0│ │-2.0(2.0) + 0.5(-1.0) │ └ -4.5 ┘ └ ┘ └ ┘ └ ┘ PEHLI row × vector → pehla number DOOSRI row × vector → doosra number

Poora hal — sample paper Q-1 ke numbers par:

Diya hua: f(x) = W₂ max(0, W₁ x) (biases nahi hain) x = ( 2.0, -1.0 ) W₁ = [ 1.5 1.0 ] W₂ = [ 0.8 -1.2 ] [-2.0 0.5 ] QADAM 1 — pre-activation z = W₁x : z₁ = 1.5(2.0) + 1.0(-1.0) = 3.0 - 1.0 = 2.0 z₂ = -2.0(2.0) + 0.5(-1.0) = -4.0 - 0.5 = -4.5 z = ( 2.0, -4.5 ) QADAM 2 — hidden activation h = ReLU(z) : h₁ = max(0, 2.0) = 2.0 ← positive tha, waisa hi raha h₂ = max(0, -4.5) = 0.0 ← negative tha, zero ho gaya (neuron "band") h = ( 2.0, 0.0 ) QADAM 3 — output f(x) = W₂h : f(x) = 0.8(2.0) + (-1.2)(0.0) = 1.60 + 0 = 1.60 JAWAB: z = (2.0, -4.5), h = (2.0, 0.0), f(x) = 1.60

Teen cheezein jo examiner dekhta hai

  • Teeno cheezein alag alag likhein — z, h, aur f(x). Sawal mein teeno maange gaye thay, sirf aakhri number likhne par poore marks nahi milenge.
  • ReLU ne ek neuron maar diya — h2 = 0. Ek line likh dein: "the second hidden unit is inactive, so its weight −1.2 contributes nothing". Ye technical justification hai, aur rubric mein isi ke marks hain.
  • "Without biases" ka khayal rakhein — is sawal mein (1, z) wala 1 chipkana nahi hai. Agar bias hota, to z = b1 + W1x hota.

Khud karein — sigmoid ke saath. Wohi x aur W1, magar ReLU ki jagah sigmoid, aur W2 = [1.0, 1.0]:

Jawab
z = (2.0, -4.5) (ye waisa hi hai) h₁ = e²/(1+e²) = 7.389/8.389 = 0.881 h₂ = e^-4.5/(1+e^-4.5) = 0.0111/1.0111 = 0.011 f(x) = 1.0(0.881) + 1.0(0.011) = 0.892

Farq dekhein: ReLU ne doosre neuron ko bilkul 0 kar diya tha (poora band). Sigmoid ne usay 0.011 banaya — taqreeban band, magar bilkul nahi. Yehi dono ka buniyadi farq hai: ReLU exactly zero deta hai (sparsity), sigmoid kabhi zero nahi deta.

G11NN vs feature engineering

Slide ka point ye hai: neural network aur feature engineering asal mein ek hi qism ka kaam karte hain — dono x se shuru kar ke, ek ke baad ek transform lagate hain. Farq sirf ye hai ke transform kaun chunta hai.

 Feature engineering (haath se)Neural network
Transform kaun chunta haiInsaan — aap khud sochte hain ke u2 daalna chahiye ya log(u)Data — training mein khud seekh liya jaata hai
ParametersBohat kam ya bilkul nahi (standardization mein sirf mean/std)Bohat zyada — har layer ka poora matrix
ShakalKoi bhi — jo aap sochein (ratio, log, polynomial, one-hot)Ek khaas shakal — hamesha "affine phir h"
Samajh aata hai?Haan — "ye column BMI hai"Nahi — hidden neuron ka koi naam nahi hota
Kab behtar haiData kam ho; domain knowledge mojood ho; jawab ki wajah batani hoData bohat zyada ho; rishta complex ho; wajah batane ki zaroorat na ho

Slide ka jumla — yaad kar lein

"We can think of NNs as doing data-driven automatic feature engineering." Aur ye bhi: NN ko aksar "automatic feature engineering" ke liye hi istemaal kiya jaata hai. Agar sawal mein poocha jaaye "how does a NN relate to feature engineering", to bas yehi likhna hai: hidden layers hi seekhe hue features hain — wo kaam jo pehle insaan haath se karta tha, ab network data se khud kar leta hai, magar is ki qeemat interpretability ki shakal mein ada karni padti hai.

G12Pre-trained neural networks

Ye seedha G11 se nikalta hai. Agar hidden layers "seekhe hue features" hain, to ek sawal banta hai: kya hum kisi aur ke seekhe hue features udhaar le sakte hain? Jawab: haan. Yehi pre-trained network hai.

Slide ka tareeqa, 4 qadam mein:

1. Ek NN ko kisi target par TRAIN karo - bohat bade data set par - bohat waqt lagta hai (shayad hafte, bade GPUs par) 2. Ab network ke saare parameters ko FIX kar do (jam do, badlo mat) 3. AAKHRI HIDDEN LAYER ka output zM-1 ∈ Rd(M-1) utha lo 4. Us zM-1 ko kisi DOOSRE kaam ke FEATURES ki tarah istemaal karo

Sab se hairat ki baat (aur yehi exam mein poocha jaata hai): slide kehti hai ye "can work well even when the other prediction tasks are quite different from the original one". Yaani doosra kaam pehle se bilkul mukhtalif ho, phir bhi chal jaata hai.

Wajah: pehli training mein network ne jo features banaye, wo kisi ek kaam ke liye makhsoos nahi thay — wo aam (general) features thay. Jaise images par train kiya gaya network pehle kinare (edges), phir shakalein, phir cheezein pehchanne lagta hai. Ye cheezein har image waale kaam mein kaam aati hain, chahe kaam billi pehchanna ho ya X-ray mein bimari dhoondna.

Do misaalein slides par hain — dono ke numbers yaad rakhein:

NaamKya karta haiOutput
word2vecEnglish ke lafzon ko vector mein badalta haiR300
VGG16Images ko vector mein badalta haiR1000

Asaan misaal

Ek aisa banda socha jaaye jo 20 saal se darzi hai. Us ne kapde ke baare mein bohat kuch seekh liya — naap lena, kapde ka girna, sutli ki mazbooti. Ab agar usay parde (curtains) banane ko kaha jaaye — kaam bilkul alag hai — magar uski buniyadi samajh wahi kaam aayegi, aur wo naye bande se kai guna tez seekhega. Pre-trained network wohi tajurbaykaar darzi hai. Hum uski "samajh" (aakhri hidden layer) le lete hain aur apne naye kaam par laga dete hain.

G13Biological neuron se perceptron tak

Ab Lecture-6 ka doosra hissa shuru hota hai — perceptron. Ye NN ka sab se purana aur sab se saada roop hai: ek neuron, aur uske seekhne ka tareeqa.

Slides mein asli (biological) neuron ki tasveer hai aur us se mila kar artificial neuron samjhaya gaya hai. Ye muqabla aksar poocha jaata hai:

Asli (biological) neuronArtificial neuron meinKaam
DendriteInputs x1, x2, …, xMDoosre neurons se signal lena
Synapse ki taqatWeights w1, w2, …, wMSignal ko kam ya zyada karna — connection strength
Cell body (soma)a = Σ xiwiSaare signals jama karna — activation level
Axon / fire karnaif (a ≥ t) output = 1 else 0Had se oopar ho to signal aage bhejna
Presynaptic terminalOutput signalAgle neuron tak pohonchana

Slide ka jumla tamam kahani keh deta hai:

Teen line — perceptron ka poora amal

  • Input signals x aur weights w ko multiply kiya jaata hai
  • Weights connection strength ko zaahir karte hain
  • Signals ko jama kiya jaata hai — agar kaafi hon, to FIRE!

Formula ki shakal mein:

M a = Σ xi wi ← activation level (pre-activation) i=1 if (a > t) output = 1 else output = 0 ← threshold t se moqabla

Slide par ek achi baat likhi hai: summation Σ se darne ki zaroorat nahi — ye "just like a loop from 1 to M" hai. Programming ki zubaan mein:

double a = 0; for (int i = 0; i < M; i++) a = a + x[i] * w[i]; // corresponding elements multiply karo, jama karo if (a > threshold) FIRE!

Ab slides ka hal shuda numerical. Diya hua: x = [1.0, 0.5, 2.0], w = [0.2, 0.5, 0.5], t = 1.0.

Q1. Activation a kya hai? a = (1.0 × 0.2) + (0.5 × 0.5) + (2.0 × 0.5) = 0.20 + 0.25 + 1.00 = 1.45 Q2. Neuron fire karta hai? a = 1.45 > t = 1.0 → HAAN, fire karta hai (output = 1) Q3. Agar threshold 0.5 kar dein aur weight #3 ko zero kar dein? a = (1.0 × 0.2) + (0.5 × 0.5) + (2.0 × 0.0) = 0.20 + 0.25 + 0.00 = 0.45 a = 0.45 < t = 0.5 → NAHI, fire nahi karta (output = 0)

Q3 ka sabaq — is par sawal ban sakta hai

Dhyan dein: threshold kam kiya gaya (1.0 se 0.5), jo fire karna aasaan banata hai. Magar neuron phir bhi fire nahi karta! Wajah: w3 = 0 karne se sab se bada contribution (1.00) khatam ho gaya. Yaani w3 hi is faisle ka asal maalik tha. Sabaq: zero weight ka matlab hai wo feature bilkul nazarandaz ho gaya — ye seedha sparsity aur feature selection (Lasso) se mil jaata hai.

G14Perceptron decision rule aur threshold ka asar

Perceptron ka faisla (decision rule) yeh hai:

┌ M ┐ if │ Σ xiwi │ ≥ t then output = 1, else output = 0 └ i=1 ┘

Ye rule do dimensions mein ek seedhi line banata hai (zyada dimensions mein hyperplane) — line ke ek taraf output 1, doosri taraf 0. Isay decision boundary kehte hain.

Slides par chaar misaalein di gayi hain, jin mein weights aur threshold badal kar dikhaya gaya hai ke boundary kaise hilti hai:

Misaalw1w2tBoundary par asar
11.00.20.05Ek rukh (orientation)
22.10.20.05w1 bada — line zyada khadi ho gayi
31.90.020.05w2 taqreeban zero — feature 2 ka asar khatam
4−0.80.030.05w1 negative — boundary ulti taraf palat gayi

Slide ka nateeja ek jumle mein: "Changing the weights/threshold makes the decision boundary move."

Aur farq samajh lein — ye poocha jaa sakta hai:

  • Weights badalne se boundary ka rukh (angle/orientation) badalta hai — line ghoomti hai. Weight ka nishaan (sign) badal dein, to line poori palat jaati hai (misaal 4).
  • Threshold badalne se boundary khisakti (shift) hai — angle wohi rehta hai, line sirf aage peeche sarak jaati hai. Threshold kam = fire karna aasaan = zyada points "1" banenge.

G15Threshold ko weight bana dena — bias trick

Threshold ko alag se sambhalna takleef deta hai. Slides par ek safai (rearrangement) ki gayi hai jis se threshold bhi ek aam weight ban jaata hai. Ye 4 qadam yaad karein — derivation poochi jaa sakti hai:

QADAM 1 shuru: Σ xiwi ≥ t QADAM 2 t ko udhar le jao: Σ xiwi - t ≥ 0 QADAM 3 -t ko (-1 × t) likho: Σ xiwi + (-1)(t) ≥ 0 QADAM 4 ab naam rakh do: x0 = -1 (hamesha -1, PERMANENT input) w0 = t (threshold hi is ka weight ban gaya) to: Σ xiwi + (x0 w0) ≥ 0 NATEEJA summation ko i = 0 se shuru kar do: M Σ xiwi ≥ 0 ← ab threshold GHAYAB hai! i=0

Slide ka jumla: "We now treat the threshold like any other weight with a permanent input of −1."

Is ka faida kya hai? Teen cheezein, aur paper mein yehi poochi jaati hain:

  • Ek hi cheez seekhni hai, do nahi. Pehle weights alag seekhne thay aur threshold alag. Ab sab ek hi vector w mein hai, to ek hi update rule sab par lag jaata hai.
  • Faisla saaf ho gaya — ab bas wTx ≥ 0 dekhna hai. Koi number yaad rakhne ki zaroorat nahi.
  • Augmented vector — is naye, ek extra entry waale vector ko augmented kehte hain. Sample paper ke Q-3 mein yehi lafz tha: "augmented weight vector".

Dhyan — −1 ya +1?

Lecture-6 ke perceptron slides mein x0 = −1 aur w0 = t liya gaya hai. Magar neural network ke layer formula mein (1, zi-1) tha — wahan +1 hai. Dono theek hain, bas nishaan ka farq hai:

x0 = -1, w0 = t → Σxiwi - t ≥ 0 x0 = +1, w0 = b = -t → Σxiwi + b ≥ 0

Yaani bias = − threshold. Aaj kal "bias" aur +1 waala tareeqa zyada chalta hai (aur sample paper Q-3 mein bhi x0 = 1 hi tha). Sawal mein jo diya ho, wohi istemaal karein — khud se badalna nahi.

G16Margin aur Perceptron Learning Algorithm

Ye OHT-II ka Q-3 hai. Do cheezein samajhni hain: margin kya hai, aur weights kaise update hote hain.

Pehle margin. Ek point i ka margin:

margini = yi ( wTxi )

Is ek chhote se formula mein poori chaalaki hai. Dekhein ye kaam kaise karta hai:

Asli label yiwTxi (model ka raw score)Margin yi(wTxi)Matlab
+1positive (+2.6)positive (+2.6)Theek — dono aik hi taraf
+1negative (−0.2)negative (−0.2)Ghalat — update chahiye
−1negative (−3.0)positive (+3.0)Theek — dono aik hi taraf
−1positive (+1.5)negative (−1.5)Ghalat — update chahiye

Margin ka poora matlab — do line

Nishaan (sign) batata hai theek ya ghalat. margin > 0 → correctly classified. margin < 0 → misclassified, update karo.
Size (magnitude) batata hai kitne yaqeen se. Bada margin = model poore yaqeen se sahi hai, aur boundary se door hai. Chhota margin = boundary ke bilkul paas, thoda sa dhakka bhi usay ghalat kar sakta hai.

Ab algorithm. Slide par bilkul yeh likha hai:

initialise weights (w) Repeat UNTIL all points are correctly classified : Repeat FOR EACH point i : margin = yi ( wTxi ) nikalo if margin > 0 : point theek hai → kuch na karo else : Δw = η yi xi w_new = w_old + Δw end end

Update rule mein tasalli ki baat ye hai ke ye apne aap kaam karta hai. Dekhein kaise — ye "why does the update work" wala sawal hai:

  • Agar yi = +1 tha aur score kam aaya, to Δw = η(+1)xi = +ηxi — yaani w ko xi ki taraf hilaya gaya. Agli baar wTxi barh jaayega.
  • Agar yi = −1 tha aur score zyada aaya, to Δw = −ηxi — w ko xi se door hilaya gaya. Agli baar score kam ho jaayega.
  • Dono surat mein margin barhta hai. Is liye slide mein likha hai: "change the weights to increase margin".

Riyazi saboot (ek line mein) — agar "prove the update increases the margin" poocha jaaye:

naya margin = yi (w_newT xi) = yi ( (w_old + ηyixi)T xi ) = yi w_oldTxi + η yi2 xiTxi = purana margin + η ‖xi‖2 (kyunki yi2 = 1) ╵╵╵╵╵╵╵╵╵ hamesha POSITIVE → margin BARHA

Ye chhoti si derivation 6 marks waale sawal mein poore marks dila deti hai, kyunki yi2 = 1 (kyunki y ∈ {−1, +1}) wali chaal hi asal nuktah hai.

Mukammal hal shuda misaal — sample paper Q-3 ke numbers:

Diya hua: w_old = [ 0.4, -0.6, 0.2 ] (w₀ bias hai, x₀ = 1 ke saath) x = [ 1.0, 2.0, 3.0 ] y = +1 η = 0.2 QADAM 1 — raw prediction field wTx : wTx = (0.4)(1.0) + (-0.6)(2.0) + (0.2)(3.0) = 0.4 - 1.2 + 0.6 = -0.20 QADAM 2 — margin : margin = y (wTx) = (+1)(-0.20) = -0.20 < 0 → margin NEGATIVE hai, yaani sample MISCLASSIFIED hai → update karo QADAM 3 — Δw = η y x : Δw = 0.2 × (+1) × [1.0, 2.0, 3.0] = [ 0.20, 0.40, 0.60 ] QADAM 4 — w_new = w_old + Δw : w_new = [ 0.4 + 0.20, -0.6 + 0.40, 0.2 + 0.60 ] = [ 0.60, -0.20, 0.80 ] VERIFICATION (zaroor likhein) : w_newTx = 0.60(1) - 0.20(2) + 0.80(3) = 0.60 - 0.40 + 2.40 = +2.60 > 0 → ab x THEEK classify ho gaya

Verification ki line kabhi na chhodein

Sample paper ke official solution mein aakhir mein verification likhi hui hai. Ye rubric ka "technical justification" wala hissa hai — sirf wnew likh dena aadha jawab hai. Ek line aur: "margin went from −0.20 to +2.60, so the point is now correctly classified".

G17Perceptron convergence theorem

Slide par theorem bilkul yun likha hai — lafz ba lafz yaad karne ke qabil hai:

Perceptron Convergence Theorem

"If the data is linearly separable, then application of the Perceptron learning rule will find a separating decision boundary, within a finite number of iterations."

Is mein do shart aur do waade hain, aur sawal inhi ke darmiyan ke farq par banta hai:

 Theorem kya kehta haiTheorem kya nahi kehta
ShartData linearly separable hona chahiye — yaani ek aisi line/hyperplane mojood ho jo dono classes ko bilkul alag kar deAgar data separable nahi hai, to kuch bhi zamanat nahi — algorithm hamesha chalta rahega, kabhi rukega nahi
WaadaEk separating boundary mil jaayegiBest boundary milegi — ye nahi kaha gaya. Jo pehle mil jaaye, wahan ruk jaata hai
WaqtFinite iterations mein — yaani kabhi khatam hogaKitne iterations — ye nahi bataya. Finite ka matlab "kam" nahi hota

Ye do nuqte bohat zyada poochey jaate hain:

  1. Data linearly separable na ho to kya hoga? Perceptron kabhi nahi rukega — wo har baar kisi na kisi point par ghalti karega aur weights hilaata rahega, hamesha ke liye. Is liye practice mein ek maximum iterations ki had lagani padti hai. Yehi perceptron ki sab se badi kamzori hai, aur isi ka ilaaj hinge loss + SVM hai (dekhein I7, I11) — kyunki wahan hum "zero ghalti" ke bajaye "jitni kam ho sake" minimize karte hain, jo har qism ke data par kaam karta hai.
  2. Perceptron best boundary nahi deta. Jis lamhe saare points theek ho gaye, wo ruk jaata hai — chahe boundary points ke bilkul saath sataayi hui ho. SVM is se behtar hai kyunki wo sab se zyada margin wali boundary dhoondta hai, sirf koi ek nahi.

Part H — Lecture 7

Classifiers

Lecture-7 ka poora maqsad ek hi sawal hai: jab jawab number na ho, balki ek "qism" ho (spam/not spam, 3 bimariyon mein se ek), to kya karein? Kyunki hamare saare models (linear predictor, neural network) sirf numbers nikaalte hain, qismein nahi. Lecture ka hal ye hai: qism ko number bana do (embed), number ka andaza lagao (predict), phir number ko wapas qism bana do (un-embed). Ye teen lafz poore Part H ka khulasa hain.

H1Categorical output aur label set

Ab tak raw output v ek number tha (ghar ki qeemat). Ab wo ek qism (category) hai. Notation:

v ∈ V , jahan V ek MEHDOOD (finite) set hai V = { 1, 2, …, K } ← K qismein; inhein CLASSES, LABELS ya CATEGORIES kehte hain

Aam tor par hum sirf V = {1, …, K} likh lete hain — yaani qismon ko number de dete hain (1 = SPAM, 2 = HAM). Dhyan: ye numbers "zyada ya kam" ka matlab nahi dete, sirf naam hain.

K ki ginti par naam badal jaata hai — ye farq exam mein zaroor aata hai:

ShartNaamMisaalein (slides se)
K = 2Boolean classificationV = {TRUE, FALSE}
V = {POSITIVE, NEGATIVE}
K > 2Multi-class classificationV = {YES, MAYBE, NO}
V = {ALBANIA, AZERBAIJAN, …}
V = {HINDI, TAMIL, …}
dictionary ke saare English lafz
ghodon ki race ke K! mumkin natije

Aakhri do misaalein kyun ahem hain

"Dictionary ke saare lafz" (K lakhon mein) aur "K! mumkin orders" (factorial, yaani 10 ghodon par 3,628,800 classes) — ye batate hain ke K bohat bada ho sakta hai. Isi liye aage one-hot embedding ke masail aate hain (K bada = vector bohat lamba), aur isi liye word2vec jaise pre-trained embeddings ki zaroorat padti hai.

H2Classifier ki definition

Categorical raw output v ∈ V ka andaza lagana, raw input u ∈ U dekh kar → isay CLASSIFICATION kehte hain Predictor ki shakal: f : U → V v-hat = f(u) hamara andaza hai v ka Is context mein f ko CLASSIFIER kehte hain.

Aur ek bohat kaam ki baat slide par hai:

Classifier asal mein kya karta hai

"Roughly speaking, a classifier partitions all u ∈ U into those with predictions v-hat = k, k = 1, …, K."

Yaani: classifier poore input space ko K tukdon mein baant deta hai. Jo input kisi tukde mein gira, usay us tukde ka label mil gaya. Ye nazariya aage Voronoi diagram (H18) mein kaam aayega — wahan ye tukde asal mein nazar aa jaate hain.

Slides ki saari applications — ek table mein. Agar paper mein "give an example of Boolean / multi-class classification" aa jaaye, yahan se koi bhi uthayein:

Applicationu (input) mein kya haiv (output)Qism
Medical diagnosisPatient ke attributes, test resultsBimari hai / nahi — ya {COVID19, FLU, COLD}Dono ho sakti hai
AdvertisingBanday ke attributes + jo ad dikhaya gayaCheez khareedi / ad par click kiyaBoolean
Fraud detectionTransaction ke attributes{FRAUD, VALID}Boolean
Image classificationEk image{LION, TREE, BUS, …}Multi-class
Spam filterEmail ke attributes{SPAM, HAM}Boolean
Sports forecastingMatch ke attributes, team A vs B{A, B, TIE}Multi-class (K=3)
Topic detectionArticle ya news item{POLITICS, SPORTS, BUSINESS, …}Multi-class
Sentence parsingEk jumlaJumle ka grammatical parsing (ek labelled tree)Multi-class (K bohat bada)

Aakhri do misaalein (sports forecasting ke saath TIE, aur sentence parsing) dikhati hain ke "class" koi bhi cheez ho sakti hai — ek lafz, ek tree, ek tarteeb — shart sirf ye hai ke mumkin jawabon ki ginti mehdood ho.

H3Error rate — sab se saada metric

Data set: u₁, …, uₙ aur v₁, …, vₙ Predictions: v-hati = f(ui), i = 1, …, n prediction CORRECT jab v-hati = vi prediction WRONG jab v-hati ≠ vi | { i : v-hati ≠ vi } | error rate e = --------------------------- n ( |S| ka matlab: set S mein kitne elements hain )

Yaani error rate sirf ye hai: kitne ghalat hue, taqseem kitne total thay. Is se zyada kuch nahi.

Do baatein slides par saath likhi hain, aur dono ahem hain:

  • Error rate sab se saada performance metric hai classifier ka.
  • Classifier ko validate karne ka tareeqa: error rate unseen ya held back (test) data par nikalein — training data par nahi. (Ye wohi baat hai jo OHT-1 ke A12 mein thi.)

Error rate kaafi kab nahi hota — ye bohat poocha jaata hai

Farz karein 1000 logon ka cancer test hai, jin mein se 10 ko waqai cancer hai. Ek bewaqoof classifier banayein jo har aik ko "cancer nahi" keh de:

errors = 10 (wo 10 cancer waale) error rate = 10/1000 = 1% → "99% accurate"!

99% accuracy, aur classifier bilkul bekaar hai — ek bhi cancer nahi pakda. Masla ye hai ke error rate donon qism ki ghalti ko barabar ginta hai, jabke haqeeqat mein cancer chhod dena (false negative) cancer ka jhoota shaq (false positive) se kai guna khatarnaak hai. Isi masle ka hal Neyman-Pearson metric hai (H7) — aur yehi wajah hai ke poora Lecture 7/8 usi ke gird ghoomta hai.

H4Chaar nataij: TP, TN, FP, FN

Boolean classification mein V = {−1, +1} liya jaata hai. Naam:

v = -1 → NEGATIVE class v = +1 → POSITIVE class

Ab (v-hat, v) ke jore ki sirf chaar mumkin surtein hain — ye table lafz ba lafz yaad karein:

NaamShartDoosra naamRoman Urdu mein
True positive (tp)v-hat = 1 aur v = 1—Positive kaha, positive tha. Theek.
True negative (tn)v-hat = −1 aur v = −1—Negative kaha, negative tha. Theek.
False negative (fn)v-hat = −1 aur v = 1Type II errorNegative kaha, magar tha positive. Chook gaye (miss).
False positive (fp)v-hat = 1 aur v = −1Type I errorPositive kaha, magar tha negative. Jhoota alarm.

Confuse na hon — naam padhne ka qaida

Doosra lafz batata hai model ne kya kaha. Pehla lafz batata hai wo sahi tha ya nahi.

False Negative | | | └─ model ne "negative" kaha └─────── magar wo GHALAT tha → asal mein positive tha

Yaani "false negative" = jhoota negative = asal mein positive tha. Is ek qaide se chaaron naam foran khul jaate hain.

Type I / Type II ko yaad rakhne ka tareeqa: Type I mein ek "I" hai → ek cheez paida kar di jo nahi thi (jhoota alarm, false positive). Type II → doosri qism, yaani jo tha usay chhod diya (miss, false negative).

Asli zindagi mein kaunsi ghalti zyada buri hai? Ye poocha jaata hai, aur jawab "depends" hai — magar wajah ke saath:

HaalatZyada khatarnaakKyun
Cancer screeningFalse negativeBimari chhoot gayi → ilaaj nahi hua → maut. Jhoota shaq sirf ek aur test karwa deta hai.
Spam filterFalse positiveZaroori email spam folder mein chala gaya → kaam ka nuqsan. Ek spam inbox mein aa jaaye to sirf delete karna hai.
Fraud detectionDonon mehngeFN = paisa gaya. FP = sahi customer ka card block, aur wo naraz.

H5Boolean confusion matrix

Chaaron ginti ko ek 2×2 matrix mein rakh dein — usay confusion matrix kehte hain. Lecture ka layout:

┌ ┐ C = │ tn fn │ │ fp tp │ └ ┘ asal: -1 asal: +1 (column = ASLI class)

Slides se chaar haqeeqatein — ye seedha sawal ban sakta hai:

tn + fn + fp + tp = n (total examples) N = tn + fp (kitne NEGATIVE examples thay) P = fn + tp (kitne POSITIVE examples thay) diagonal (tn, tp) → THEEK predictions ki ginti off-diagonal (fn, fp) → GHALAT predictions, dono qism ki

Transpose ka masla — dhyan rakhein

Lecture-7 khud ye warning deti hai (multi-class waale hisse mein): "warning: some people use the transpose of C". Yaani kuch kitabon mein rows aur columns ulte hote hain. Exam mein hal yeh hai: apne matrix ke saath rows/columns par label likh dein (jaise "rows = predicted, columns = actual"). Phir chahe aap ka layout examiner se ulta ho, aap ka jawab saaf aur sahi rahega. Numbers se khud pehchanne ka tareeqa: tn aur tp aam tor par sab se bade hote hain, aur wo diagonal par hone chahiye.

H6Saare performance metrics — ek jagah

Slides metrics ko do hisson mein baantte hain: buniyadi (basic) teen, aur "error measures some people use" — yaani jo log aksar istemaal karte hain. Dono padh lein, kyunki sawal mein kisi ka bhi naam aa sakta hai.

Buniyadi teen:

NaamFormulaMatlab
False positive ratefp / NAsli negatives mein se kitne ko ghalti se positive kaha
False negative ratefn / PAsli positives mein se kitne chhoot gaye
Error rate(fn + fp) / nKul ghalti

Baaqi metrics — in ke kai kai naam hain, aur paper mein kisi bhi naam se aa sakte hain:

Naam (saare naam)FormulaSlide ka matlab
True positive rate = sensitivity = recalltp / P"Fraction of true positives we correctly guess"
False alarm ratefp / N"Fraction of true negatives we incorrectly guess as positive"
Specificity = true negative ratetn / N"Fraction of true negatives we correctly guess"
Precisiontp / (tp + fp)"Fraction of our positive guesses that really are positive"

Recall vs Precision — sab se zyada confuse karne wali jodi

Farq sirf denominator (neeche wala) ka hai. Ye ek line yaad kar lein:

RECALL = tp / P ← neeche: jo ASLI mein positive thay → "jo thay, un mein se kitne pakde?" PRECISION = tp / (tp+fp) ← neeche: jo HUM NE positive kaha → "jo pakde, un mein se kitne sach thay?"

Machhli pakadne wali misaal. Talaab mein 100 machhliyan hain. Aap ne jaal daala aur 30 cheezein nikaleen, jin mein 24 machhliyan thin aur 6 joote.
Recall = 24/100 = 24% — "talaab ki machhliyon mein se kitni nikaaleen" (kitni reh gayi).
Precision = 24/30 = 80% — "jo nikaala us mein se kitna machhli thi" (kitna kooda aaya).

Donon ko aik saath barhana mumkin nahi hota — ye trade-off wala sawal hai. Agar aap recall barhane ke liye bada jaal daalein (zyada cheezon ko "positive" kehna shuru karein), to joote bhi zyada aayenge, yaani precision gir jaayegi. Aur agar precision barhane ke liye sirf bilkul yaqeeni cases ko positive kahein, to bohat si asli machhliyan reh jaayengi, yaani recall gir jaayega.

Hal shuda misaal — saare metrics ek confusion matrix se. Farz karein:

┌ ┐ C = │ tn=80 fn=5 │ │ fp=10 tp=5 │ └ ┘ n = 80 + 5 + 10 + 5 = 100 N = tn + fp = 80 + 10 = 90 (asli negatives) P = fn + tp = 5 + 5 = 10 (asli positives) false positive rate = fp/N = 10/90 = 0.111 = 11.1% false negative rate = fn/P = 5/10 = 0.50 = 50% error rate = (fn+fp)/n = 15/100 = 0.15 = 15% recall / sensitivity = tp/P = 5/10 = 0.50 = 50% specificity = tn/N = 80/90 = 0.889 = 88.9% precision = tp/(tp+fp) = 5/15 = 0.333 = 33.3%

Is misaal ka sabaq: error rate sirf 15% hai, jo acha lagta hai — magar recall sirf 50% hai, yaani aadhe asli positives chhoot gaye. Agar ye cancer test hota, to ye classifier bilkul na-qaabil-e-qubool hota. Yehi wajah hai ke sirf error rate dekhna khatarnaak hai.

H7Neyman-Pearson metric (Boolean)

Masla: hamare paas do metrics hain (false positive rate aur false negative rate), aur dono ko chhota karna hai. Magar "best" model chunne ke liye hamein ek number chahiye — do numbers par muqabla nahi ho sakta.

Hal: dono ko ek weight ke saath jama kar do. Isay Neyman-Pearson metric kehte hain:

κ · fn + fp NP = -------------------- n κ > 0 (kappa) = false negatives ki, false positives ke muqable mein, kitni fikar hai

κ ka asar — ye teen surtein yaad karein, sawal inhi par banta hai:

κMatlabClassifier ka rawayya
κ > 1False negatives zyada pareshan karte hainShaq hone par bhi "positive" keh dega — kuch chhodna nahi chahta (cancer test)
κ < 1False negatives kam pareshan karte hainPoore yaqeen ke bagair "positive" nahi kahega (spam filter)
κ = 1Dono barabarNP = e — bilkul error rate ban jaata hai

Ek line jo zaroor likhein

"When κ = 1, the Neyman-Pearson error reduces to the overall error rate." Ye jumla taqreeban har NP waale sawal mein 1 mark ka hissa hota hai, aur Lecture-8 mein bhi dobara aata hai.

Ab is ki geometry. Slides par ek graph hai: x-axis par fn/n, y-axis par fp/n. Har classifier is plane par ek point hai. Aur (κfn + fp)/n = constant ki har value ek seedhi line banati hai:

Slide ye bhi batati hai ke NP error asal mein kya hai: "a scalarization of two objectives". Yaani do maqsadon ko ek number mein daba dena. Jo bhi do cheezein ek saath chhoti karni hon, unhein weight de kar jama karna — yehi scalarization hai. (Ye wahi tareeqa hai jo RERM mein tha: loss + λ × regularizer.)

H8Multi-class error types — ginti ka hisaab

Boolean mein sirf 4 surtein thin. Multi-class mein zyada hain, aur ginti ka hisaab exam mein poocha jaata hai:

v, v-hat ∈ {1, …, K} To (v-hat, v) ke jore ki mumkin values = K² In mein se: CORRECT : v-hat = v → K surtein (diagonal) ERROR : v-hat ≠ v → K² - K = K(K-1) surtein

Aur slide ki ahem baat: hum in ghaltiyon ko aik hi cheez nahi ginte, balki har jode (j, k) ke liye alag qism ki ghalti maante hain:

v-hat = j, v = k (jahan j ≠ k) → "hum ne k ko j SAMAJH LIYA (mistook k for j)" → asal value k thi, magar hum ne j ka andaza lagaya

Misaal se dekhein. K = 3, classes {COVID19, FLU, COLD}:

K² = 9 surtein kul Correct (3): COVID→COVID, FLU→FLU, COLD→COLD Errors (6): COVID ko FLU samjha ← khatarnaak COVID ko COLD samjha ← bohat khatarnaak FLU ko COVID samjha ← sirf pareshani FLU ko COLD samjha COLD ko COVID samjha ← sirf pareshani COLD ko FLU samjha

Ab wajah saaf hai ke kyun har ghalti alag ginni padti hai: COVID ko COLD samajhna aur COLD ko COVID samajhna — dono "ek ghalti" hain, magar nuqsan zameen aasmaan ka farq hai. Pehli surat mein marz phail jaayega; doosri surat mein banda khamakha ghar baith jaayega. Is liye multi-class mein κ ek number nahi, ek poora vector hota hai (H10).

H9Multi-class confusion matrix

C ek K × K matrix hai: Cjk = kitne records mein v-hat = j AUR v = k row j = jo hum ne KAHA (predicted) column k = jo ASAL mein tha (actual)

Slides ki saari properties — har aik seedha sawal ban sakta hai:

BaatMatlab
C ki saari entries jama = nHar record theek ek khaane mein girta hai
Column sums → har class mein kitne records thayColumn k ka total = asli class k ke records ki ginti
Ckk (diagonal)Class k ko kitni baar theek pehchana
Cjk for j ≠ kKitni baar k ko j samajh liya

Error rates — do level par:

(1) Har jode ka apna error rate — K(K-1) alag alag rates: ejk = Cjk / n , j ≠ k (2) Overall error rate — saare off-diagonal jama: e = Σ Cjk / n = 1 - Σ Ckk / n j≠k k ╵╵╵╵╵╵╵╵╵╵╵╵ 1 - (diagonal jama / n)

Sab se tez tareeqa — exam mein yehi karein

Overall error rate nikalne ke liye off-diagonal ke 6 ya 12 numbers jama karna waqt ki barbadi hai aur ghalti ka khatra. Hamesha diagonal jama karein (trace), aur ghata dein:

error rate = 1 - (trace of C) / n jahan trace = C₁₁ + C₂₂ + … + CKK (diagonal ke numbers jama)

Diagonal par sirf K numbers hote hain, off-diagonal par K(K−1) — yaani K = 5 par 5 numbers jama karne hain, 20 nahi.

Hal shuda misaal — Lecture-8 ki Iris matrix (do features ke saath):

┌ ┐ │ 50 0 0 │ n = 150 (har class ke 50 plants) C = │ 0 38 13 │ │ 0 12 37 │ └ ┘ trace = 50 + 38 + 37 = 125 error rate = 1 - 125/150 = 25/150 = 0.1667 = 16.67% Padhein kya ho raha hai: - Column 1 (setosa): 50 theek, 0 ghalat → 100% perfect - Column 2 (versicolor): 38 theek, 12 ko class 3 samjha - Column 3 (virginica): 37 theek, 13 ko class 2 samjha NATEEJA: setosa bilkul alag hai (aasaan). Versicolor aur virginica aapas mein ghul mil gaye hain — saari 25 ghaltiyan inhi do ke darmiyan hain, aur TAQREEBAN BARABAR dono taraf (12 aur 13).

Is "padhne" wale hisse par 6 marks ka sawal ban sakta hai: "interpret the confusion matrix". Jawab mein teen cheezein likhein: (1) error rate ka number, (2) kaunsi class aasaan thi, (3) kaunsi do classes aapas mein confuse hui aur kya wo symmetric confusion hai (yahan haan — 12 vs 13, taqreeban barabar).

H10Multi-class Neyman-Pearson metric

Boolean mein κ ek number tha. Multi-class mein ye ek vector ban jaata hai — har class ke liye ek weight.

Pehle ek nayi cheez define hoti hai:

rk = Σ Cjk ← class k ko kitni baar KISI DOOSRI class samjha j≠k (yaani column k ka total, minus Ckk) rk/n = class k ko ghalat samajhne ka error rate

Phir in saare rk ko weights ke saath jama kar dein:

K NP = Σ κk rk / n k=1 jahan κ = (κ₁, κ₂, …, κK) ek weight vector hai, saari entries NONNEGATIVE κk = class k ko ghalat samajhne ki hamein kitni fikar hai Agar κk = 1 for ALL k → NP = error rate

Boolean aur multi-class — saath mein

BOOLEAN : NP = (κ · fn + fp) / n κ ek NUMBER hai κ = 1 → error rate MULTI-CLASS : NP = Σk κk rk / n κ ek VECTOR hai saare κk = 1 → error rate

Dono mein wohi ek baat: jo ghalti zyada mehngi hai, uska weight bada rakho. Aur dono mein wohi ek check: saare weights 1 kar dein to simple error rate wapas aa jaata hai.

Misaal — medical diagnosis. V = {COVID19, FLU, COLD}. COVID ko kuch aur samajhna sab se khatarnaak hai (marz phail jaayega), COLD ko kuch aur samajhna sab se kam nuqsan deta hai:

κ = ( 10 , 3 , 1 ) ╵╵ ╵ ╵ COVID FLU COLD Matlab: COVID chhodna = 10 guna COLD chhodne se bura FLU chhodna = 3 guna COLD chhodne se bura NP = ( 10 · rCOVID + 3 · rFLU + 1 · rCOLD ) / n

Is se training mein kya hoga? Model COVID ki taraf jhuk jaayega — shaq hone par wo COVID keh dega, kyunki COVID chhodne ki saza 10 guna hai. Natija: COVID ka recall barhega, magar kuch sehatmand logon ko bhi COVID keh diya jaayega. Aur yehi hum ne chaha tha — κ ka maqsad bilkul yehi hai.

H11Embedding the output — representative aur constellation

Ab lecture ke asal nuqte par aate hain. Masla: hamare predictors (linear, neural network) sirf numbers nikaalte hain. Magar jawab ek qism hai — "SPAM". Model "SPAM" nahi likh sakta.

Hal: qism ko ek number (ya vector) bana do.

Raw output v ∈ V ko Rm mein embed karo: y = ψ(v) ψ (psi) EMBEDDING function hai, ψ : V → Rm

Kyunki V mein sirf K cheezein hain, is liye ψ ko poora likhne ki zaroorat nahi — bas K vectors bata dein:

y₁ = ψ(1), y₂ = ψ(2), …, yK = ψ(K) Do naye naam (dono exam mein aa sakte hain): yk → class k ka REPRESENTATIVE { y₁, y₂, …, yK } → CONSTELLATION

Do naam, aasaan matlab

Representative = us class ka "numberon waala pata" — wo jagah jahan wo class rehti hai. Constellation = saare pataon ka majmua (jaise aasman par sitaron ka jhurmat — is liye ye naam). Model ka kaam ab ye hai: aise numbers nikaalo jo sahi class ke pate ke qareeb hon.

Slides ki saari misaalein — ye dekhein ke embedding kitni mukhtalif ho sakti hai:

EmbeddingmNote
TRUE → 1, FALSE → −11Sab se aam Boolean embedding — Lecture-8 isi par bani hai
TRUE → 1, FALSE → 01Ye bhi chalta hai, magar ±1 zyada aasaan hai (dekhein neeche)
YES → 1, MAYBE → 0, NO → −11Teen classes, sirf ek number — kyunki in mein tarteeb hai
YES → (1,0), MAYBE → (0,0), NO → (0,1)2Reduced one-hot — MAYBE default hai
APPLE → (1,0,0), ORANGE → (0,1,0), BANANA → (0,0,1)3One-hot — koi tarteeb nahi, sab barabar
(Horse 3, Horse 1, Horse 2) → (3,1,2)3Race ka order — dikhata hai class koi bhi cheez ho sakti hai
word2vec — 10 lakh lafz → R300300Seekha hua embedding (G12)

Sab se zaroori baat: ek number waali embedding (m=1) sirf tab theek hai jab classes mein waqai tarteeb ho. YES > MAYBE > NO mein tarteeb hai, is liye 1, 0, −1 theek hai. Magar {APPLE, ORANGE, BANANA} ko 1, 2, 3 kehna ghalat hoga — is se model samajh lega ke ORANGE "APPLE aur BANANA ke darmiyan" hai, jo bemani hai. Isi masle ka hal one-hot hai. (Ye wohi baat hai jo OHT-1 ke B3 — faithful embedding mein thi.)

H12One-hot aur reduced one-hot embedding

One-hot embedding — K classes ko RK mein daalein:

yk = ek , k = 1, …, K ( ek = k-waan unit vector = sirf position k par 1, baaqi sab 0 ) Boolean (K=2) ke liye: y₁ = (1, 0) , y₂ = (0, 1) K=3 ke liye: y₁ = (1,0,0) , y₂ = (0,1,0) , y₃ = (0,0,1)

Reduced one-hot embedding — wohi kaam, magar RK−1 mein (ek dimension kam):

1. Ek class ko DEFAULT chun lo → usay 0 ∈ RK-1 par bhej do 2. Baaqi (K-1) classes ko unit vectors e₁, …, eK-1 ∈ RK-1 par bhej do Boolean (K=2) ke liye: one-hot : y₁ = (1, 0) , y₂ = (0, 1) → R² reduced one-hot : y₁ = 0 , y₂ = 1 → R¹ Slide ki misaal — V = {MAYBE, YES, NO}, default = MAYBE: ψ(MAYBE) = (0, 0) ψ(YES) = (1, 0) ψ(NO) = (0, 1)
 One-hotReduced one-hot
Dimension mKK − 1
Classes barabar hain?Haan — saari classes ek jaisi (symmetric)Nahi — ek class "default" hai, baaqi us se naapi jaati hain
FaidaSaaf, symmetric; argmax se un-embed hota hai (H16)Ek parameter column kam — K bada ho to bachat
NuqsanEk extra dimension; entries hamesha 1 par jama hoti hain (redundancy)Default class ka rawayya mukhtalif — un-embedding rule peecheeda ho jaata hai (H17)

Reduced one-hot ki ek dimension kaise bachti hai? Kyunki one-hot mein ek dimension bekaar hai. Dekhein: agar aap ko pata ho ke pehli K−1 entries kya hain, to aakhri entry khud bata deti hai (kyunki sab ka total 1 hota hai). Yaani ek dimension ki koi nayi maloomat nahi — wo redundant hai. Reduced one-hot bas usay hata deta hai.

Lecture-8 ke Australian weather example mein isi ka zikr hai: wahan likha hai "note 16 one-hot embedded values for WindDir9am all sum to one" — yehi redundancy hai.

H13Embed → predict → un-embed

Ye poore Lecture-7 ka khulasa hai. Slide ke lafz: "In words: embed → predict → un-embed". Teen qadam:

QADAM 1 EMBED raw input u ko feature vector x bana do x = φ(u) aur raw output v ko representative y bana do y = ψ(v) QADAM 2 PREDICT ek predictor banao jo x se y ka andaza lagaye g : Rd → Rm , y-hat = g(x) umeed: y-hat taqreeban y ke barabar ho (yaani ‖y-hat - y‖² chhota ho) QADAM 3 UN-EMBED y-hat (numbers) ko wapas class bana do v-hat = ⌜ y-hat ⌟ ⌜·⌟ : Rm → V UN-EMBEDDING function hai

Teeno ko jama kar ke poora classifier banta hai:

v-hat = f(u) = ⌜ g( φ(u) ) ⌟ ╵╵╵╵ ╵╵╵╵╵ ╵╵╵╵╵╵╵ un-embed predict embed (QADAM 3) (QADAM 2) (QADAM 1) (Padhein ANDAR se BAHAR — φ pehle, ⌜·⌟ aakhir mein)

Parametrized predictor. Amal mein g ko ek parameter θ ke saath likha jaata hai:

gθ : Rd → Rm θ wo cheez hai jo hum CHUNTE hain (training) Classifier: v-hat = fθ(u) = ⌜ gθ(φ(u)) ⌟ θ ko ERM se chunte hain (training data par) Phir VALIDATE karte hain — performance metric, TEST data par

Slides mein teen qism ke parametrized predictors gine gaye hain — ye table yaad rakhein:

Predictorθ mein kya haiy-hat kahan se aata hai
Tree-based
(classification tree)		Tree ka structure, har node par kaunsa feature aur kaunsa threshold, aur har leaf ki valueUs leaf ki value jahan point pohonchta hai
Neural networkHar layer ke offsets (bias) aur weightsAakhri layer ka output
Linear predictorEk d × m matrixy-hat = g(x) = θTx

H14Nearest neighbour un-embedding

Model ne y-hat diya — numbers. Ab usay class banana hai. Tareeqa bilkul saada hai:

v-hat = ⌜ y-hat ⌟ = arg min ‖ y-hat - yk ‖₂ k Roman Urdu mein: "dekho y-hat kis class ke REPRESENTATIVE ke sab se QAREEB hai, wohi class bata do"

Slide ek chhoti si zaroori baat bhi kehti hai: "we can break ties any way we like" — yaani agar y-hat do representatives se bilkul barabar faasle par ho, to jo marzi chun lein; is se koi farq nahi padta (kyunki aisa hona bohat kam-yaab hai).

Asaan misaal

Socha jaaye teen shehron ke pate hain: Karachi (0,0), Lahore (10,8), Islamabad (11,12). Aap ka GPS kehta hai aap (9.5, 8.4) par hain. Aap kaunse shehar mein hain? Jawab: jo sab se qareeb hai — Lahore. y-hat wohi GPS reading hai, representatives wohi shehar ke pate hain, aur un-embedding wohi "sab se qareeb kaunsa hai" ka sawal hai. Bas itna.

Ab aage ki teen topics (H15, H16, H17) sirf yehi dikhati hain ke mukhtalif embeddings ke liye ye "sab se qareeb" wala rule kis shakal mein khul jaata hai — aur hairat ki baat ye hai ke har baar wo bohat saada ban jaata hai.

H15Boolean un-embed — sign rule

Embed: TRUE → y₁ = 1 FALSE → y₂ = -1 Un-embed: v-hat = TRUE, jab y-hat ≥ 0 FALSE, jab y-hat < 0

Ye nearest-neighbour rule se kaise nikla? Ye derivation poochi jaa sakti hai, aur ek line ki hai:

+1 aur -1 ke beech ka DARMIYAN (midpoint) = 0 y-hat > 0 → +1 se qareeb → TRUE y-hat < 0 → -1 se qareeb → FALSE

Sab se ahem nateeja — yaad rakhein

±1 embedding ke saath, un-embedding sirf sign(y-hat) hai. Isi liye Lecture-8 mein decision boundary y-hat = 0 hoti hai — har jagah. Aur isi liye margin = y · y-hat itna kaam ka hai: y aur y-hat ka ek hi nishaan ho to product positive → prediction theek. Teen cheezein (±1 embedding, sign un-embedding, aur margin) ek hi system ke teen hisse hain.

H16One-hot un-embed → argmax (saboot ke saath)

One-hot embedding ke saath nearest-neighbour rule ek bohat khoobsurat shakal le leta hai:

v-hat = arg min ‖ y-hat - ek ‖₂ → v-hat = arg max y-hatk k k Yaani: "y-hat ki SAB SE BADI entry dekho, uska number hi class hai"

Ye kaise saabit hota hai? Slide par poora saboot hai aur ye 6 marks ka sawal ban sakta hai. Teen line:

‖ y-hat - ek ‖₂² = ‖y-hat‖₂² + ‖ek‖₂² - 2 y-hatk ╵╵╵╵╵╵╵╵╵ ╵╵╵╵╵╵╵ ╵╵╵╵╵╵╵ k par depend = 1 hamesha k par depend NAHI karta (k par depend KARTA hai nahi karta) Pehle DO terms k par depend nahi karte — har k ke liye wohi hain. To minimize karne ke liye sirf (-2 y-hatk) ko chhota karna hai, yaani y-hatk ko BADA karna hai. NATEEJA: arg min ‖y-hat - ek‖₂ = arg max y-hatk k k

Do chhoti baatein jo saboot mein zaroori hain (aur jin par marks katte hain):

  • ‖ek‖22 = 1 har k ke liye — kyunki unit vector mein ek hi 1 hai, aur 12 = 1. Is liye ye term constant hai.
  • y-hatTek = y-hatk — kyunki ek se dot product karne par sirf k-waan entry bachti hai, baaqi 0 se multiply ho kar khatam ho jaati hain.

Kyun ye itna ahem hai

Pehle un-embedding ke liye K distances nikaalni padti thin (har aik mein square root aur jama). Ab sirf sab se bada number dhoondna hai — jo ek nazar ka kaam hai. Isi liye har asli multi-class system (softmax, neural network classifiers) argmax hi istemaal karta hai. Aur dhyan dein — ye wahi cheez hai jo J6 (multi-class logistic loss) mein phir aayegi.

Misaal. K = 3, one-hot, aur model ne diya y-hat = (0.2, 0.7, 0.1):

Tareeqa 1 — distances nikaalein (lamba rasta): ‖(0.2,0.7,0.1) - (1,0,0)‖² = 0.64 + 0.49 + 0.01 = 1.14 ‖(0.2,0.7,0.1) - (0,1,0)‖² = 0.04 + 0.09 + 0.01 = 0.14 ← sab se chhota ‖(0.2,0.7,0.1) - (0,0,1)‖² = 0.04 + 0.49 + 0.81 = 1.34 Tareeqa 2 — argmax (chhota rasta): sab se badi entry = 0.7 → position 2 DONO ka jawab: v-hat = class 2

Dono tareeqon ka jawab aik hai — magar doosra tareeqa 10 guna tez hai. Exam mein argmax wala tareeqa likhein, aur ek line mein wajah bhi likh dein (ke do terms k par depend nahi karte).

H17Reduced one-hot un-embed (yes/maybe/no)

Reduced one-hot mein un-embedding rule peecheeda ho jaata hai — yehi is embedding ki qeemat hai. Slide ki misaal:

Embed: YES → (1, 0) MAYBE → (0, 0) ← default class, origin par NO → (0, 1) Un-embed (y-hat = (y-hat₁, y-hat₂) ke saath): ┌ YES, jab y-hat₁ > y-hat₂ AUR y-hat₁ > 1/2 │ v-hat = │ MAYBE, jab y-hat₁ < 1/2 AUR y-hat₂ < 1/2 │ └ NO, jab y-hat₂ > 1/2 AUR y-hat₁ < y-hat₂ (boundaries par koi bhi value chun lein)

Ye 1/2 kahan se aaya? Nearest-neighbour rule se. (0,0) aur (1,0) ke darmiyan ka perpendicular bisector y-hat1 = 1/2 par hai — yaani aadhe raste par. Isi tarah (0,0) aur (0,1) ke beech y-hat2 = 1/2. Aur (1,0) vs (0,1) ke beech ki boundary y-hat1 = y-hat2 hai (diagonal). Teen representatives → teen boundaries.

H18Voronoi diagram

Ab in saare ilaaqon ko ek naam de dein. Nearest-neighbour un-embedding poore space Rm ko K tukdon mein baant deti hai:

Rm ko in regions mein baanta jaata hai: Rk = { y-hat : ⌜y-hat⌟ = k } , k = 1, …, K yaani: Rk = wo saari jagahein jahan jawab "class k" aata hai Is poori taqseem ka naam: VORONOI DIAGRAM

Slides se is ki teen khaas baatein — ye definition-type sawal ka jawab hain:

BaatMatlab
Regions polyhedra hainSeedhe kinaron waali shakalein — koi curve nahi, koi gol kinara nahi. ("Sets of points closer to one representative than to all others.")
Boundaries perpendicular bisectors hainDo representatives yj aur yk ke beech ki boundary — unhein jorne wali line ke theek darmiyan, aur us par 90° par
Boundaries hyperplanes hainYaani affine (seedhi) — is liye hisaab aasaan rehta hai

Asli zindagi mein Voronoi

Mobile network ke towers socha jaaye. Aap ka phone hamesha sab se qareebi tower se judta hai. To poora shehar khud ba khud tukdon mein baant jaata hai — har tower ka apna ilaaqa. Ye naqsha bilkul Voronoi diagram hai. Isi tarah school ki zoning (bachcha sab se qareebi school jaata hai) bhi Voronoi diagram hi hai.

H19Classifier ka loss function

Ab training ki taraf. Loss function batata hai ke ek ghalti kitni buri hai:

ℓ : Rm × V → R ℓ(y-hat, v) = jab asli value v thi, aur hum ne y-hat kaha, to hamein kitna bura laga

Ab ek chhoti magar bohat kaam ki baat: kyunki v ki sirf K mumkin values hain, is liye poora loss function likhne ki zaroorat nahi — bas K functions bata dein:

ℓk(y-hat) = ℓ(y-hat, k) , k = 1, …, K ℓk(y-hat) = "jab asli class k thi, to y-hat kehna kitna bura tha" Yaani: ek loss function = K alag alag curves (har class ke liye ek)

Aur acha loss kaisa hona chahiye? Slide ki shart:

ℓk(y-hat) hamesha NONNEGATIVE ho ℓk(y-hat) CHHOTA ho jab y-hat taqreeban yk ke barabar ho

Sab se saada loss — square loss:

ℓ(y-hat, v) = ‖ y-hat - ψ(v) ‖₂² yaani: prediction aur sahi representative ke darmiyan faasle ka square

Slides khud warning deti hain

Lecture-7 do baar yehi jumla dohraati hai: "we'll see far better loss functions for classifiers later". Yaani square loss sirf shuruati misaal hai — ye classification ke liye waqai bura hai. Kyun bura hai, ye poora Lecture-8 ka mauzoo hai (dekhein I3), aur yehi OHT-II ka Q-4 bhi hai. Is liye agar sawal mein "why is square loss undesirable for classification" aaye, to jawab I3 mein hai.

H20ERM, RERM aur least squares classifier

Ye bilkul wahi cheez hai jo OHT-1 ke Part D mein thi — sirf ab classification ke saath. Agar ERM bhool gaye hain, ye do line kaafi hain:

Diya hua: training data x₁,…,xₙ aur v₁,…,vₙ aur ek parametrized predictor gθ EMPIRICAL RISK = saare points ka AVERAGE loss: 1 n 1 n L(θ) = --- Σ ℓ( gθ(xi), vi ) = --- Σ ℓv(i)( gθ(xi) ) n i=1 n i=1 ERM : aisa θ chuno jo L(θ) ko sab se chhota kare RERM : aisa θ chuno jo L(θ) + λ r(θ) ko sab se chhota kare ╵╵╵╵╵╵╵ r = regularizer, λ > 0 hyper-parameter

Slide ye bhi kehti hai: "in most cases, we need to resort to numerical optimization to find θ" — yaani formula se seedha jawab nahi nikalta, computer se dhoondna padta hai. Magar ek surat mein nikalta hai, aur wohi least squares classifier hai:

Least squares classifier — teen cheezon ka majmua

(1) LINEAR predictor : y-hat = θTx (2) SQUARE loss : ℓ(y-hat, v) = ‖y-hat - ψ(v)‖₂² (3) SQUARE regularizer : r(θ) = ‖θ‖₂² NAAM: "least squares classifier"

Iska sab se bada faida: slide kehti hai "can solve the RERM problem exactly using least squares" — yaani numerical optimization ki zaroorat nahi, formula se seedha jawab mil jaata hai (closed form). Iska sab se bada nuqsan: wohi jumla phir aata hai — "we'll see better loss functions for classifiers later".

Teen classifiers ke naam — ye table poore course mein sab se zyada poocha jaata hai. (Tafseel I10 mein hai, magar yahan se shuru hota hai.)

NaamLossRegularizer
Least squares classifierSquare lossSquare
Logistic regressionLogistic lossKoi bhi
Support vector machine (SVM)Hinge lossSquare

H21NP metric ke liye ERM — loss ko κ se scale karna

Ek masla reh gaya. Hum ne kaha ke hum Neyman-Pearson metric ki fikar karte hain (κ ke saath). Magar training mein hum loss minimize karte hain, metric nahi. To κ training mein kaise ghusega?

Jawab bilkul seedha hai: loss ko κ se multiply kar do.

Agar ℓk(y-hat), k = 1,…,K hamare BAGAIR-WEIGHT (unweighted) losses hain, to un ki jagah ye istemaal karein: ℓkweighted(y-hat) = κk · ℓk(y-hat) , k = 1, …, K Slide ke lafz: "to reflect different costs for different errors, we SCALE THE LOSSES by κ"

Yaani: jis class ko chhodna mehnga hai, us class ke points par loss bada kar do. Phir optimizer khud ba khud un points ko zyada ahmiyat dega — kyunki wahan ghalti se risk zyada barhta hai.

Slides ki Boolean misaal:

Boolean classifier, embedding y₁ = -1, y₂ = +1 Hum NP metric (κ fn + fp)/n ki fikar karte hain κ > 0 = false negatives ki, false positives ke muqable mein, kitni fikar Loss function: ┌ ‖y-hat - y₁‖² jab v = -1 (normal weight) │ ℓ(y-hat,v) = │ │ └ κ · ‖y-hat - y₂‖² warna (v = +1) (κ guna weight) Slide: "which gives MORE WEIGHT to deviating from the positive representative y₂"

Poora rishta — ye zanjeer yaad rakhein

METRIC (jo hum naapte hain) LOSS (jo hum minimize karte hain) NP = (κ fn + fp)/n <--------> positive class ka loss × κ ╵ ╵ TEST data par TRAINING mein validate karte hain istemaal hota hai

Yaani κ do jagah aata hai, aur dono jagah wohi matlab rakhta hai: positive class ki ghalti kitni mehngi hai. Agar sawal mein κ diya ho, to poochein: ye training (loss) mein use ho raha hai ya validation (metric) mein? Aksar dono mein.

Slides mein aage ek example graph bhi hai (square loss, sum-squares regularizer ke saath): bayein plot par training errors blue aur test errors red dikhaye gaye hain, aur dayein plot par minimum-error classifier. Nateeja wohi aam qaida: λ ya κ ko validation se chunein, training error se nahi.

Lecture-7 ka mukammal khulasa — slides ke Summary slide se

  • Classifier wo predictor hai jiska raw output categorical ho: V = {1,…,K}.
  • K = 2 → Boolean classifier; K > 2 → multi-class classifier.
  • Inhein mukhtalif error rates se naapa jaata hai, jo confusion matrix mein jama hote hain — aur ye hamesha test data par.
  • Fitting ERM ya RERM se hoti hai: raw output ko ψ se embed karein (yk = ψ(k) class k ka representative hai), embedded output ke liye predictor banayein, phir y-hat ko nearest-neighbour rule se un-embed karein.
  • Classifiers ke liye makhsoos loss functions hain — wo Lecture-8 mein aate hain.
